## Current critical path (per Mike, 2026-04-30)

The queue has three lanes after the context rebuild lands. Lane order is sequential; do not start the next until the previous is at a stable stopping point.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: UI `.pdui` clean-boot source registration (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the fresh boot UI log instead of accepting the render-loop repair as normal. The universal loader still scanned UI before texture configs existed, then the render loop auto-emitted 15 missing `.pdui` archives. `.pdui` emission/scan now reruns once after `texReset()` builds `g_TexGeneralConfigs`, registers all 15 UI archives through the universal walker, marks the catalog dirty, and reloads theme textures from those `.pdui` sources before the render repair path. Verification passed source guard, strict generated-output conformance with all 27 families and no `.tsv` entries, focused source-gate static coverage, isolated client build, and `boot_smoke` with log proof `written=15 scanned=15 registered=15` and no `PDGUI theme: 15 of 15 ... missing` line.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: B-798 mesh part-table preservation (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the fresh boot extraction discrepancy through the `.pdmesh` builder instead of treating the "OBJ export produced no triangles" log as the root cause. The real failure point was `romextract_pdmesh.c::s_writePartTableJson()`: three real title/training model archives (`base_model_nlogo3.pdmesh`, `base_model_pdfour.pdmesh`, `base_model_pdone.pdmesh`) had native part-table entries that did not resolve to registered node ids, and the extractor aborted the entire archive. Extraction now preserves those unresolved links in editable `model.parts.json` as `node=-1` / `node_unresolved=true`, still writes node/part JSON for rootless-but-sane modeldefs, treats zero-triangle exports as non-fatal diagnostics, and bumped the mesh fast-cache key so stale skipped archives regenerate. Verification produced all 733 mesh jobs with `failed=0`; strict generated conformance now passes 8,060 root / 9,061 checked archives across all 27 families; and the three formerly skipped mesh archives contain OBJ/MTL plus nodes/parts/faces/render JSON.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: Catalog-provider mesh marker alignment (`all-family parity`)**: After B-798, the focused catalog-provider static test still failed on the old `.pdmesh` fast-cache marker even though generated archives and strict conformance were current. The test now pins the v23 `zero_tri_models` mesh cache marker so coverage matches the extraction shape being loaded by the game. Verification passed the focused `[catalog][provider][static]` selector with 11,091 assertions / 33 cases, source guard, and narrowed diff check.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: No-public-TSV spot audit (`all-family parity`)**: Rechecked active public TSV risk after Mike reaffirmed TSV is effectively bin-like junk for modders. Current checked examples and the generated smoke-install archive tree have no `.tsv` entries; the narrowed active-code scan found no public TSV emit/load writer. Remaining TSV strings are guard/conformance forbidden-entry lists, stale-cache cleanup/migration cleanup, deliberate bad-archive tests, and historical Kanban/context text.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: MP3 configured speech playback config parity (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the configured MP3 alias path beyond extraction and into live playback. The archive path was now typed `.pdvoice`, but `sndStartMp3()` still used the alias-row index as an audio-config index after resolving through `g_AudioRussMappings`. Playback now resolves `audioconfig_index` before applying configured volume, pan, offensive-filter, and respond-hello flags. Verification passed source guard, focused catalog/c3844 tests with 18,438 assertions / 53 cases, isolated client build, and exact-client `all_family_source_gate_smoke` 36/36 with configured audio and UI source-only loads.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: UI `.pdui` catalog apply parity (`all-family parity`)**: The all-family smoke showed `.pdui` archives existed and the theme loaded `base:ui_bg_alt`, but direct `catalogLoadTypedAsset(ASSET_UI, "base:ui_bg_alt")` still failed. The failure point was `pdgui_theme.cpp`: the catalog UI apply callback re-registered catalog-owned UI rows after loading their texture, wiping the public FileProvider source handle. Catalog UI apply now only loads textures into the theme cache; loose mod texture imports remain the registration path. Exact-client `all_family_source_gate_smoke` now passes 36/36 and logs `result type=ui id='base:ui_bg_alt' result=OK`.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: Weapon diagnostic log gating (`all-family parity`)**: Runtime asset-parity triage found no fresh source-only or ROM-fallback-after-extraction failure in the latest smoke log, but plain `boot_smoke` still emitted unconditional `LOG.WPN.DIAG` weapon instrumentation from older debugging. `sysLogPrintf()` now suppresses that diagnostic stream unless a test explicitly asks for weapon diagnostics with `--debug-weapon-diag`, `--debug-force-first-person`, or `--debug-generated-mesh-render-audit`, preserving the weapon source-gate mesh/first-person proof while keeping unrelated asset tests readable. Verification passed focused static log coverage, source guard, isolated client build, `boot_smoke` with zero `LOG.WPN.DIAG` matches, and `weapon_match_source_gate_smoke` with expected generated mesh render and first-person weapon proof.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: Configured MP3 speech alias extraction (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the audio extraction warnings from the boot log instead of treating `.pdsfx` output counts as enough. The failure point was `romextract_pdsfx.c::s_emitConfiguredAliasSounds()`: configured SFX aliases whose mapped target is MP3/file-backed speech were decoded as sound-bank leaves, so file IDs such as 1914 were reported as outside the 1545-entry bank. The extractor now detects mapped `mp3priority` aliases, skips nonexistent `.pdsfx` bank archive emission, and packages those sources as typed `.pdvoice::sample.mp3` archives with `source_filenum` binding. Runtime MP3 playback and prop-sound duration resolve the catalog/provider typed voice source before the old loose extracted bridge or ROM/static fallback. Boot smoke now reports 104 configured MP3 alias voice archives checked, `romextract pdvoice ... failed=0`, and no MP3 source-only, PDSFX alias loud-fail, or maps-outside-sound-bank logs.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: B-797 Scenario path native ID parity (`all-family parity`)**: The next full generated-output conformance run failed on `.pdscenario::navigation/paths.json` because validation and runtime loading still assumed `path_ref == row_index`. That was a bad contract: extraction was correctly preserving native `paths[i].id`, while the public row order preserves the physical native path table order used by pointer-order consumers. Some native stages are sparse and `base_scenario_mp_skedar` / `base_scenario_extra6` duplicate `path_0000`, so `path_ref` is native data rather than a unique row key. Conformance now accepts readable `path_NNNN` refs that fit the native u8 range without sorting or deduping rows, and the runtime path loader no longer rejects `row.id != table->count`. Strict conformance over the smoke install now passes all 8,057 root / 9,058 checked archives across all 27 typed families; focused c3844/c3842/pdmod static tests and the Chicago source smoke also pass.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: No-public-TSV cleanup follow-up (`all-family parity`)**: Mike clarified that TSV files are effectively opaque ripped row dumps and do not fit the accessible-files concept. The no-public-TSV constraint remains strict: public typed archives use semantic JSON/INI/graph or standard editable media/source files, while TSV may appear only as stale-archive detection, migration-forensics text, or deliberate bad-archive tests. This slice removed dead in-memory Scenario `tiles_tsv` and `materials_tsv` diagnostic ledgers from extraction, updated `scenario_pads_source_gate_smoke` so Chicago runtime proof requires JSON Scenario/mission source paths, narrowed static tests so stale TSV rejection remains allowed without treating TSV as active source, and tightened the native-source guard so `.tsv` is no longer a public text-source suffix.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: Scenario source-scene camera delivery (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the Chicago source-scene runtime path from archive activation through the native source renderer instead of treating extraction output as sufficient. The failure point was runtime camera delivery: the public `.pdscenario::scene.glb` activated with geometry/materials/textures, but `scenarioSceneRendererSetCameraFrame()` rejected the frame because the VI FOV was `0.000`, so no native source-scene draw happened. `bgRenderScene()` now feeds the source renderer sanitized projection inputs from the live player camera path, falling back through player FOV, zoom FOV, and 60 degrees only when the VI value is unusable. The renderer logs one-shot accepted/rejected camera proof, the Chicago smoke now requires accepted camera and rendered native source-scene lines while forbidding rejected/missing-camera paths, and verification passed source guard, focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844],[modding][pdxxx][c3842]`, isolated builds, and `scenario_pads_source_gate_smoke` 154/154. Remaining all-family parity work still includes broader material/combiner/fog parity, animation timing/skeleton verification, audio edge cases, and representative mesh/model usage proof beyond this Scenario camera bridge.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: B-769 generated mesh hit-test pointer parity (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the remaining mesh/runtime parity thread through the live render path instead of only archive shape. Current `.pdmesh` archives already emit OBJ/MTL plus semantic `model.nodes.json`, `model.parts.json`, `model.faces.json`, and `model.render.json`; the confirmed failure point was a missed character hit-test walker in `propobj.c` that still reconstructed generated display-list pointers as native segmented model bytes. Generated source modeldefs now use the real `rwdata->gdl` and `rodata->dl.xlugdl` pointers in that walker while native modeldefs keep the old segmented path. Verification passed source guard, focused B-769 static coverage, focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844],[modding][pdxxx][c3842]`, isolated client/tests builds, and `weapon_match_source_gate_smoke` with generated-mesh render audit enabled. Remaining all-family parity work still includes broader material/combiner/fog parity, animation timing/skeleton verification, audio edge cases, Scenario camera matrices, and representative body/head/title/prop usage proof beyond this hit-test path.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: Chicago source-scene room-list parity (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the remaining Chicago source smoke warning after source `scene.glb`, `portals.json`, and native room tables activated. The warning was not another missing archive source field: `bgFindEnteredRooms()` wrote valid full room lists with the `-1` terminator in slot 7, but its validation only scanned slots 0-6 before declaring the list malformed. The runtime now scans the reserved terminator slot, the Chicago source smoke forbids the old `BG.ROOMS: entered room list missing terminator` warning, and the rerun passed 149/149 while proving source native portal and background room tables.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: Scenario source-scene material alpha parity (`all-family parity`)**: Followed the remaining Chicago visual/material mismatch where source geometry loaded but textures appeared dark or gridded. The public `scene.glb` PNGs already carried alpha, but extracted materials did not declare alpha behavior and the native source renderer ignored texture alpha, so transparent texels rendered as opaque dark pixels. Extraction now marks alpha-bearing GLB materials with `alphaMode=MASK` / `alphaCutoff=0.01`, source renderer activation logs alpha texture/material counts, and the shader discards transparent output pixels. Chicago source smoke now proves `alpha_textures=39 alpha_materials=38 uv=TEXCOORD_1 alpha=mask` and passes 150/150. Remaining all-family parity work still includes broader material/combiner/fog parity, animation timing/skeleton verification, audio edge cases, and representative mesh/model usage paths beyond Scenario source-scene alpha.

**LATEST 2026-06-07: B-793** fixes the next audio parity gap found after the keymap/pitch pass. Extracted `.pdsfx` / `.pdvoice` archives already carried loop markers, but base archive walkers, external/distributed scanners, and `sndStart()` discarded them and file playback queued WAV bytes once with no live handle. Audio source rows now preserve `has_loop`, `loop_start_samples`, `loop_end_samples`, and `loop_count`; `sndStart()` starts `audioStartFileSound()` with those fields; and the port audio bridge mixes source WAV handles each frame so gameplay can stop, pan, repitch, and loop source-backed SFX/voice through the existing handle API. Verified with source guard, Python compile for the guard, diff check, isolated `audioloop` client/test builds, and focused `[catalog][provider][static],[modding][pdxxx][base][static][c3812]` with 11,913 assertions / 46 cases. Remaining audio parity risk is envelope/release/fx behavior, which should be tested by playtest rather than assumed complete.

**LATEST 2026-06-07: B-794** fixes the next confirmed audio flattening gap. Native `ALSound` playback uses `ALEnvelope` attack/decay/release values and keymap velocity fields, but `.pdsfx` / `.pdvoice` extraction preserved only pitch/pan/volume/loop data. Extraction now emits readable `velocity_min`, `velocity_max`, `has_envelope`, `attack_time_us`, `decay_time_us`, `release_time_us`, `attack_volume`, and `decay_volume` fields in `sound.ini` / `voice.ini` and the manifest; stale audio archives missing `attack_time_us` or `velocity_max` regenerate; catalog walkers/scanners/net distribution preserve those fields for base and mod archives; and `audioStartFileSound()` applies attack/decay scaling plus release fades for live source-backed handles. Verified with source guard, Python compile for the guard, diff check, isolated `audioenv` client/updater and test builds, and focused `[catalog][provider][static],[modding][pdxxx][base][static][c3812]` with 11,922 assertions / 46 cases. Remaining audio parity risk is native fxmix/high-bit keymap chaining and any audible edge cases found in playtest.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-07: Scenario animation usage source proof (`all-family parity`)** closes the concrete proof gap where Scenario animation actions resolved catalog IDs but did not require the public `.pdanim` payload before using the native animation number. `chr_do_animation`, `set_camera_animation`, `object_do_animation`, `do_preset_animation`, `if_natural_anim`, and special-death resolution now require a compiled public animation clip via `catalogLoadTypedAsset(ASSET_ANIMATION, ...)` / `catalogGetLoadedAnimationClip()` before applying or comparing the animation. Live action logs include `anim_source` and `clip_bytes`, and Scenario source-matrix/static guard coverage requires `.pdanim::animation.gltf` proof for representative camera, character, and object actions. Attempted live CI Training/Rescue Scenario source-matrix smoke did not reach the game because shared `Build\data\ntsc-final\scenarios` contains stale archives missing current JSON members such as `navigation/waypoints.json`; refresh extracted Scenario archives before using that smoke as runtime evidence. Remaining `.pdanim` proof is representative weapon/inventory command playback through weapon users and broader animation parity beyond these Scenario consumers.

**COMPLETE 2026-06-06: B-769 `.pdmesh` archive render parity follow-up**: Closed the broad generated-mesh render failure Mike reported. The failure was not isolated to one model category: public `.pdmesh` packages carried OBJ, MTL, nodes, parts, and face-to-matrix IDs, but they did not carry the per-render-node GBI/display command stream that normal `modelRender()` expects. Extraction now emits semantic `model.render.json` render commands beside OBJ/MTL/nodes/parts/faces, including named material switches, matrix commands, pop-matrix commands, matrix flags, and triangle draw order. The compiler requires that source for generated source modeldefs and rebuilds runtime display lists from it instead of inventing a flat triangle list; stale `model.render.tsv` is forbidden by conformance. Cache/schema keys were bumped, and the prior visual Chicago smoke proved this render-command class fixes coherent scene geometry instead of screen-filling broken mesh polygons. Verification for the original render parity passed isolated `b769stream` all/tests builds, focused source archive tests, `python tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, the narrow render smoke (`PASS 13/13`), and visual capture at `.claude\scratch\b769-capture-render-stream-20260606-163855\screen-42s.png`; the JSON replacement passed isolated `b772meshjson` tests/client builds, focused source archive tests, focused c3842 tests, conformance py-compile, and the native-source guard. Retained caveat: three extracted `P*` logo-ish mesh files still warn as no-triangle entries.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-06: B-770 audio archive behavior parity (`all-family parity sweep`)**: Fixed the first confirmed non-mesh parity gap from Mike's broader all-asset-type audit. `.pdsfx` / `.pdvoice` extraction now preserves native `ALKeyMap` pitch fields (`key_min`, `key_max`, `key_base`, `key_detune`) and stale archives without `key_base` regenerate. Audio archive walkers, external INI scanning, and network distribution preserve the optional keymap/sample fields without splitting base and mod handling. `sndStart()` now applies the archive's native base pitch, gameplay pitch, sample volume, and sample pan before `audioPlayFileSound()` queues the public WAV source, and `audioPlayFileSound()` resamples with the effective pitch instead of always treating file-backed SFX/voice as pitch 1.0. Verification passed source guard, isolated `assetaudio` tests/client builds, and focused static tests. Current regenerated smoke install audit: `1545/1545` `.pdsfx/.pdvoice` archives contain `sample.wav` and `key_base`, with `1487` non-default key bases and `538` non-default sample volumes. Chicago `combat_sim_chicago_match_start` loaded and rendered public source Chicago (`SCENARIO.RENDER ... vertices=19992 ... textures=98`) and regenerated audio, but failed before scripted exit with an access-violation exit code while running `--no-sound`; treat that as a separate smoke/shutdown caveat, not proof against the audio fix. Remaining all-family parity work: repeat this evidence pattern for animation timing/skeletons, Scenario materials/textures, song/MP3 duration/playback parity, UI/font/lang render behavior, and model/mesh/character/weapon usage points where source presence is not enough.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-06: B-771 Scenario scene room-shading parity (`all-family parity sweep`)**: Fixed the adjacent Scenario visual gap behind the Chicago texture/material concern. The archive already carried `scene.glb` images and DCC/runtime UV channels, but the source renderer ignored the native room shade/color payload and drew `texture(u_Tex, v_Uv)` only. Extraction now emits `COLOR_0` in generated Scenario/arena GLBs, the source renderer multiplies textures by that archived channel, and conformance requires the v9 exporter stamp plus `COLOR_0`. The initial `COLOR_0` attempt proved the archive shape but produced all-white Chicago data; root cause was a flattened color-span assumption, now fixed by bounding the lookup from the actual block color pointer inside the inflated room buffer. Verification passed source guard, texture contract across 264 generated scene GLBs, focused Scenario tests, isolated `scenecolor` tests/client builds, and Chicago v9 payload inspection: all 92 primitives carry `COLOR_0`, with `19129/19992` non-white vertices in both smoke install and `Build\data`. Remaining all-family parity work: continue the same extraction/runtime-translation audit for animation timing/skeletons, material/combiner/fog state, UI/font/lang render behavior, and any mesh/model usage points where source presence is not enough.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-06: B-772 semantic source parity (`all-family parity sweep`)**: Fixed the confirmed animation source-path and extraction-loss issues for character/cutscene `.pdanim`: extraction now emits semantic `animation.gltf`, including native `pd_anim_flags`, repeat ranges, and cut-skip frame metadata instead of public header/frame TSV byte tables. Weapon/inventory `.pdanim` archives now emit semantic `commands.json` instead of opcode payloads, and `.pdmesh` render parity source now uses `model.render.json` command objects instead of the former `model.render.tsv` bridge. The compiler restores animation GLTF extras into the private native animation stream, decodes weapon animation command objects, and rebuilds source modeldefs from named mesh render commands. Runtime animation proof passed `base_animation_source_probe_smoke` 19/19 at `.claude\smoke-verify-runs\results-20260607T004333Z.json`; mesh JSON verification passed isolated `b772meshjson` tests/client builds, focused source archive tests, focused c3842 tests, conformance py-compile, and the native-source guard. Remaining all-family parity work: prove representative body/head/weapon/camera animation users, audit fresh extracted weapon/inventory command archives, and replace remaining public TSV bridge formats in UI/font/lang/scenario-style source surfaces.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-05: B-768 .pdsong sequence source playback and modelcatalog handle lookup (`c3844-s5`; superseded for source shape by B-773)**: Continued the c3844 audio/provider audit after B-767. This slice first made sequence-only `.pdsong` rows source-native by compiling the then-current event source into the existing compact sequencer buffer after proving sibling public MIDI source. B-773 later replaced that event bridge with semantic `sequence.json`. **LATEST 2026-06-05: B-767** adds source-native MP3 speech playback and duration sizing before ROM/static fallback. `sndStartMp3()` now resolves packed MP3 file numbers through `romExtractRelPathForFilenum()`, loads the extracted public file with `fsFileLoad()`, keeps that buffer alive for `mp3PlayFile()`, and frees it on stop, replacement, or natural completion. `psGetDuration60()` now sizes MP3 duration from the same extracted public file path before any ROM file-size fallback. In `ASSET_AUDIO` source-only mode, missing extracted MP3 source fatals before ROM/static address or size reads. c3844 remains open for Scenario-last vehicle path, cover commands, route-to-target pad preset, broader Scenario parity, and any further family-owned fallback gaps found by audit.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-05: B-767 MP3 public-source playback and duration (`c3844-s5`)**: Continued the non-Scenario audio audit after B-766. The remaining packed MP3 paths had only source-only refusal guards: playback called `fileGetRomAddress()`, `fileGetRomSize()`, and `mp3PlayFile()` after the guard, and duration estimation still needed ROM file size outside source-only verification. Added `sndMp3LoadPublicSourceFile()` / `sndMp3ResolveSourceOrFallback()` in `src/lib/snd.c` so extracted public MP3 bytes feed the existing PC MP3 decoder before ROM fallback, with explicit source-buffer ownership. Updated `psMp3DurationGetSourceOrFallbackSize()` in `src/game/propsnd.c` so duration math uses the extracted public file size first. Verification passed `python tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, `python -m py_compile tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, Kanban JSON validation, isolated `c3844mp3src` `all` client/updater build, isolated `c3844mp3src` `tests` build, and focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844],[modding][pdxxx][c3842]` tests with 7,610 assertions / 25 cases.

**LATEST 2026-06-05: B-766** adds source-native `.pdsong` track-audio playback before sequencer fallback. `catalogResolveMusicSequence()` now exposes FileProvider paths for cataloged music rows, `seqPlay()` calls `modSequencePlayAudioSource()` before `modSequenceLoad()`, and public `track.wav`, `track.ogg`, or `track.mp3` sources play through the existing streaming music mixer. If that public audio source fails in `ASSET_AUDIO` source-only mode, the runtime fatals instead of falling through to legacy sequence or ROM/static bytes. Sequence-only public `.pdsong` work was still guarded in this slice and was superseded by B-768, then by B-773 for semantic JSON event source. c3844 remains open for Scenario-last vehicle path, cover commands, route-to-target pad preset, broader Scenario parity, MP3 public-source playback, and any further family-owned fallback gaps found by audit.

**PROGRESS 2026-06-05: B-766 .pdsong track-audio source playback (`c3844-s5`)**: Continued the non-Scenario audio audit after B-765. The `.pdsong` format already permits public stream tracks (`track.wav`, `track.ogg`, `track.mp3`), and the port already had a native streaming mixer for mod music, but sequenced music startup did not route cataloged public track-audio source before checking legacy sequence files and ROM sequence bytes. Added `modSequencePlayAudioSource()` in `port/src/mod.c`, made `seqPlay()` try it before `modSequenceLoad()`, and made `catalogResolveMusicSequence()` expose the catalog FileProvider path. The source guard/static tests now pin the stream-first ordering and the source-only fatal on stream-load failure. Verification passed `python tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, `python -m py_compile tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, Kanban JSON validation, isolated `c3844song` `all` client/updater build, isolated `c3844song` `tests` build, and focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844],[modding][pdxxx][c3842]` tests with 7,595 assertions / 25 cases. The first isolated client log exposed a pre-existing missing `sysFatalError` declaration in `propsnd.c`; this slice added the required include and the rerun built cleanly.

**LATEST 2026-06-05: B-765** closes the file-source SFX/voice playback failure fallback for source-only audio verification. `sndStart()` already routed public `.pdsfx` / `.pdvoice` FileProvider entries through `audioPlayFileSound()`, but if WAV decode/playback failed it logged a warning and fell back to ROM/static sound playback. **PROGRESS 2026-06-04: B-746 Scenario setup-link per-kind matrix proof (`c3844-s5`)**: Followed B-745 by auditing the remaining special setup proof surface. The matrix detected setup behavior-link rows, but the live registration assertion was generic `kind=[a-z_]+`, and runtime only logged the first setup-link registration with a single `setup_link_logged` flag. **FIXED 2026-06-01: Scenario boot-stage range includes Extra25/Extra26 (`B-506`, `c3844-s3`)**: The Scenario source matrix correctly caught a stale startup cap: `--boot-stage` rejected stage ids above `0x5d`, so `base:scenario_extra25` and `base:scenario_extra26` fell back through title flow and loaded CI Training instead of their public `.pdscenario` archives. **FIXED 2026-06-01: Large Scenario public pads compile with wide source offsets (`B-505`, `c3844-s3`)**: `base_scenario_maiansos.pdscenario` has enough public `pads.tsv` rows to push the compiled pad record zone beyond the original `u16` packed-offset table. Runtime previously treated that as a source compile failure, then correctly refused ROM pad fallback under Scenario source-only mode. Source-built padfiles now keep legacy `u16` offsets for small/legacy padfiles but register a stage-lifetime `u32` offset table when public source requires it; all pad consumers use a shared accessor. Verification passed `python tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, focused Scenario source-gate/static matrix tests with 2,253 assertions / 3 cases, isolated all-target build, and `base:scenario_extra5,base:scenario_maiansos` matrix PASS 44/44 with Maians SOS compiling 1,384 pads from public `pads.tsv` and logging the 32-bit source-offset path.

**FIXED 2026-06-01: Scenario matrix handles padded stage hex and natural stage completion (`B-504`, `c3844-s3`)**: The first broader matrix batch proved `base:scenario_extra1` and `base:scenario_extra2` loaded all required public Scenario source products, but the harness initially failed because logs zero-pad single-digit stage hex (`0x06` vs generated `0x6`) and these early test stages can complete/shut down before the scheduled scripted exit. The matrix runner now accepts zero-padded stage hex and no longer requires `SMOKE: result=scripted_exit` when all source-chain proofs are present and timeout/fatal/source-fallback patterns remain forbidden. Verification passed `python tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, focused matrix static test with 24 assertions, and `base:scenario_extra1,base:scenario_extra2` matrix PASS 44/44.

**FIXED 2026-06-01: Explicit empty Scenario source tables are valid source (`B-503`, `c3844-s3`)**: `base_scenario_test_ash.pdscenario` intentionally carries public `pads.tsv` and `portals.tsv` with headers but no rows. Runtime previously treated row count zero as missing source and tried to fall back to ROM pads/portals, which correctly failed in Scenario source-only mode. Runtime now accepts present empty public tables as authoritative empty source, compiles an empty native padfile/portal table, logs the source path, and never uses ROM fallback for that case. **Tooling note (2026-05-19)**: Kanban board status-tab refactor shipped. Active Kanban now displays one status at a time (`Backlogged`, `Active`, `Blocked`, `Done`) with a scoped dropdown for `All` and status-scoped pillars only. Cards flow in a responsive grid, sorted with starred cards first, then numbered priorities from highest to lowest, then the rest by board order. Daily Flow is now a top-level tab beside Bug Tracker and starts collapsed by default. Pending-completion Review panel title-focus polish remains shipped. B-336 release auto-commit hook compatibility is patched in `release.ps1` and Dev Window v2. B-337 v0.0.199 signature sidecar was re-signed/re-uploaded with the key embedded in the shipped updater; future sign attempts now fail if key/header mismatch. B-338 updater cleanup now protects root-level BYOR ROM files (`*.z64`, `*.v64`, `*.n64`) in both apply paths; already-published v0.0.199 and v0.0.200 predate the fix and should be superseded by a newer prerelease, then retired by the rolling prerelease prune. B-342 mission-start crash fix is build-verified and pending playtest: solo stage loads now route through an MP scenario stage-owner wrapper so Combat Simulator drop-in/drop-out is not gated on `normmplayerisrunning` alone. B-343 follow-up mission-start crash fix is now build/smoke-verified: dynamic prop collision meshes detach before stage-pool reset, the detach pass walks only live props, prop reset initializes `colmesh`, `mission_intro_flow` passes, and `auto_campaign_first_cycle` completes without crash/exception. B-344 F6 campaign-complete hotkey is second-pass build-verified: F6 completes all difficulty-active loaded solo campaign objectives, clears death/abort, calls `mainEndStage()`, and the final Skedar Ruins ImGui endscreen action is labeled Credits and routes through `endscreenContinue(2)` instead of Challenge/next-mission flow; the bot-freeze fallback is dev-build-only plus Combat Simulator-only.

**Tooling note (2026-05-20)**: Kanban card `c121` now has a dedicated Decision Requests top-level tab planned/implemented in this slice: it surfaces unresolved questions and Mike's answered responses from the same `open_questions[]` ledger. New sessions must check Mike's decision responses before choosing work unless the newest user message specifically overrides that flow; the CLI surface is `python tools/kanban_evaluator.py list-decision-requests`.

**Tooling note (2026-05-21, Memory Review + Codex automations)**: Kanban card `c3825` is active/pending Mike browser retest. The Bug Tracker data layer was cleared of stale rows `B-318` through `B-326`, and the UI now has a permanent bug delete action. `Memory Review` is dynamic now: `GET /api/memory-review` parses live `# Task Group:` entries from `C:\Users\mikeh\.codex\memories\MEMORY.md` (mirrored at `tools/kanban/memories.md`) and treats `tools/kanban/memory-review.json` as only the review-markup overlay. The tab can save `Keep` / `Adjust` / `Remove` statuses, stores adjustment notes against stable task-group ids, and has an Apply markup action that deletes `Remove` blocks and replaces `Adjust` blocks only when the note contains a full replacement `# Task Group:` block. Codex app automations now replace the old Claude morning flow: `pd2-daily-flow` runs daily at 5:00 AM and `pd2-architecture-review` runs daily at 6:00 AM.

Dev Window Pull now fetches remote commits first and lets Mike choose the commit to fast-forward to.

**Tooling note (2026-05-26, Dev Window v2 release hook)**: B-372 is fixed pending Mike release retry. The 2026-05-26 Dev Window v2 log showed Release compiled successfully, then failed at the pre-release commit because Git Bash selected MSYS Python and the c3842 pre-commit guard was launched with a mixed `/c/.../C:/.../asset_native_source_guard.py` path. `.githooks/pre-commit.py` now invokes the guard from repo-relative `tools/asset_native_source_guard.py` under repo cwd, and Dev Window v2 no longer retries failed sync commits with `--no-verify`; static c3842 coverage pins both rules.

**Tooling note (2026-05-22, Kanban priority layout)**: Kanban `c3833` is done. Active Kanban now uses a two-pane layout: numbered card titles on the left with pillar color/status metadata, selected-card contents on the right with fixed scroll boxes for description and subtasks, and bottom-docked Delete/Cancel/Save actions. Manual drag order in the left list updates `cards[].order` and is the default prioritization surface; `priority` and `flag` remain attention metadata unless auto-sort is enabled. A docked `AI Notes` modal saves Mike-authored notes under root `x_special_notes` in `tools/kanban/state.json`.

**Tooling note (2026-05-24, remote Kanban phone link)**: Kanban `c3836` is active/pending Mike phone retest. Dev Window v2 now has one-click `Start Kanban Server` and `Stop Kanban Server` utility buttons beside `Open Kanban`; Start runs `devtools/start-kanban-remote.ps1` in the background, copies/shows the generated phone URL as soon as the helper writes it, and Stop calls the tracked `-Stop` path for the remote server and tunnel. Remote mode binds the Kanban HTTP server to `127.0.0.1`, keeps local port `7531` reserved for normal tokenless use, prefers remote port `7533`, chooses the next free localhost port if needed, requires `KANBAN_REMOTE_TOKEN`, disables idle shutdown for the remote session, and exposes only that localhost service through `cloudflared tunnel --url <local-kanban-url>`. Local `Open Kanban` now explicitly requires tokenless `/api/state`; if a token-gated remote server is listening it skips that port and starts/opens a separate tokenless local board on `7531` or the next free local port. Remote mode must not enable WARP, set a system proxy, configure an exit node, or modify Windows route tables. The phone breakpoint hides desktop tabs/filter/sidebar, shows only the card list plus a Filters modal, opens tapped cards as full-screen editors, supports save/delete and Move Up/Move Down, and exposes `Start Session`. Codex sessions now have a full-screen phone view with a response-only transcript, queued prompts with selected-prompt steering, plan-mode choice-question buttons, and a message composer that resumes the same Codex thread for follow-up turns; the `Sessions` button can start ad-hoc sessions without a card. Card-session prompts include the target card, other Active cards, and other running sessions; ad-hoc prompts include Active cards and running sessions so untracked/external tasks still launch with project awareness. Logs stay under `.claude/scratch/kanban-sessions/` for diagnostics, but the phone UI hides command/stdout/stderr/raw/thread-marker noise. Parser checks, local token/cookie smoke, mobile DOM smoke, sessions endpoint smoke, choice-question extraction smoke, queue/steer backend smoke, response-only transcript smoke, and no-Codex launch/message unit smokes passed; Mike still needs to click the button with `cloudflared.exe` available and open the copied link from a phone on a different network.

**Tooling note (2026-05-24, card-specific decision workspace)**: Kanban `c3839` is done. The old `Asset Decisions` tab is now labeled `Card Decisions` and stores selected-card progress/context/memory/recommendation/decision records under `x_card_task_context.cards[card_id]`. Switching the selected card stashes the previous card's record instead of deleting it. The prior asset-format intake data remains intact under `x_asset_archive_decisions` and is copied into the `c3824` card workspace; `c3838` is the default active workspace for the extraction implementation card.

**Tooling note (2026-05-21, pd-tests loader popup)**: B-355 got a second-pass fix after Mike reported repeated `clock_gettime64` loader dialogs from automated tests. `pd-tests.exe` still imports `clock_gettime64` through MinGW winpthread, so CMake now copies the matching `libwinpthread-1.dll` beside the test binary, Dev Window v2 passes the canonical MinGW environment into Run Tests, and `run-pd-tests-smoke.ps1` now sources the build prelude and suppresses loader popups. Focused `[smoke][build][static][b355]`, direct `pd-tests.exe`, tests meta-smoke, and client/updater all-target verification passed.

**FIXED 2026-06-01: Weapon no-sound SFX references no longer serialize as fake catalog IDs (`B-482`, `c3844-s1`)**: Generated `.pdweapon` archives now serialize zero/no-sound fire `shootsound` values as `0` instead of `SFX_0000`, so loader_pool no longer logs `LOADER.POOL.WEAPON.RESOLVE_FAIL` for intentional silent fire modes such as Laser secondary. Strict conformance rejects stale `_meta/manifest.json` payloads that contain `SFX_0000`, and `.pdweapon` cache markers advanced to `embedded.v13` / `pdweapon_embedded_v13_clean_public` so Build and smoke-install archives regenerate. Verification passed Python tool compile, native-source guard, isolated `c3844sfx0` test build, focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844],[modding][pdxxx][c3842]` 2,651 assertions / 14 cases, normal `devtools\build-headless.ps1`, boot smokes for `Build` and shared install, strict generated-output conformance across both generated trees, texture-contract scans, direct `.pdweapon` archive scans with zero `SFX_0000` matches, and latest client-log scans with zero `SFX_0000` / weapon resolve-fail matches.

**FIXED 2026-06-07: Weapon command sounds resolve configured audio aliases from public source (`B-778`, `c3844`)**: Nested `.pdanim` command archives inside `.pdweapon` were emitting readable configured SFX IDs such as `base:sfx_door_8012`, but the base audio catalog did not register those configured aliases and the weapon loader accepted only `AUDIO_CAT_SFX`. The game ran, but reload/weapon command audio logged `LOADER.POOL.WEAPON.RESOLVE_FAIL` and could lose native audioconfig behavior if flattened to a leaf sample. `catalogReadableSoundRefId()` now preserves configured sound refs, the base audio catalog registers configured SFX aliases back to their native packed sound numbers, weapon audio resolution accepts any `ASSET_AUDIO` category, and public `.pdweapon` binding files are now `bindings/animations.json` / `bindings/audio.json` with no public TSV or raw numeric sound refs. `.pdweapon` cache markers advanced to `embedded.v14` / `pdweapon_embedded_v14_clean_public`. Verification passed Python compile, `python tools\asset_native_source_guard.py`, isolated `b778sfx` all-target builds, focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844][weapon][static]` 83 assertions / 1 case, focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844][source][static]` 406 assertions / 2 cases, `[catalog][provider][static]` 11075 assertions / 33 cases, and `weapon_match_source_gate_smoke` with zero fresh `LOADER.POOL.WEAPON.RESOLVE_FAIL` lines in `pd-client.log`. Remaining full conformance gap: weapon/inventory `.pdanim` command archives still need semantic catalog IDs for local include/random animation commands and standalone declared source for configured SFX alias archives; the whole-tree conformance scan correctly remains red on that broader `.pdanim` source-contract work.

**FIXED 2026-06-01: Empty base ROM slots no longer log false extraction errors (`B-483`, `c3844-s1`)**: First-launch extraction and hash verification now treat a null `romdataFileGetData(fileNum)` as an empty/skipped slot before asking for `romdataFileGetSize(fileNum)`, preventing expected unused file slots such as 2014-2047 from flooding fresh boot logs with `romdataFileGetSize: could not load file num ...`. Static coverage pins the skip-before-size-lookup order. Verification passed Python tool compile, native-source guard, isolated `c3844empty` test build, focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3844],[modding][pdxxx][c3842]` 2,654 assertions / 15 cases, normal `devtools\build-headless.ps1`, Build boot smoke, strict generated-output conformance across `Build\data\ntsc-final`, texture-contract scan, and latest Build log zero-match checks for the old file-number errors, `SFX_0000`, weapon resolve fails, fatal, and access violation lines.

**Connectivity/social note (2026-05-22)**: Kanban `c3828` has a second-pass code/build fix and remains pending Mike+Chris live retest before closure. First pass fixed per-agent presence signature validation, loaded save-slot agent names, LAN bootstrap to UDP 27105, stale-offline Invite visibility, NAT-aware invite/group-session/spectator handoffs, and the Main Menu Play cleanup. Second pass fixed the live retest gap for existing agents: `prefsAgentLoad()` brought the social hub online only on the no-sidecar path, so existing agents with prefs sidecars could mark presence loaded without opening P2P/presence sockets. Existing sidecar loads now call `socialHubBringOnline()` before `presenceMarkAgentLoaded()`. Verification: isolated `online367` `[c3828]` passed 44 assertions / 3 cases, and isolated `online367` all-target build passed. Manual retest remains: Mike and Chris load their normal existing agents, both rows become online within the presence window, wrong code still reports no user, Invite can be sent even from a stale Offline row, accept/join reaches the host session, and return flows back through Social/lobby/room.

**Campaign crash note (2026-05-21)**: B-365 is code/build/smoke verified and pending Mike playtest. The latest build exception in `Build/logs/game client/pd-client.log` symbolicated to `chrTickRobotAttack()` after the campaign auto-runner reached Infiltration (`stage=0x2f`). Robot muzzle flash now initializes/guards its model rwdata, robot attack starts only with valid robot skeleton/fireslots/beams, and attack ticking fails closed when chr/model/prop/target/gun-position state is incomplete. Verification: focused `[chraction][robot][static][b365]`, queued all-target build, and exact `auto_campaign_infiltration_robot_attack` smoke passed with no access violation/exception/fatal. Manual retest remains: replay the same build path that threw the exception and confirm Infiltration/robot-heavy progression no longer crashes.

**Falcon 2 weapon stretch note (2026-05-22)**: B-366 is code/build/smoke verified and pending Mike visual retest. The extracted Falcon 2 OBJ stretch was an exporter issue: `.pdmesh` OBJ export ignored weapon model matrix commands, so export now applies `G_MTX` / `G_POPMTX` model matrices from `SPSEGMENT_MODEL_MTX`, writes `obj_export_version = model_obj_mtx_v8` / `export_version.txt`, and bumps `.pdweapon` to `embedded.v9` / `pdweapon_embedded_v9` so standalone and nested held meshes rebuild. The final exporter pass also culls Falcon-only detached transformed effect geometry from `FILE_GFALCON2` / `FILE_GFALCON2LOD`, removing the skewed barrel-end group without applying that cull to other weapons/devices. Mike then confirmed the stretch still happened in-game with the tip staying fixed while the model moved; that live artifact was the Falcon laser-sight beam far endpoint being projected from the crosshair while the near endpoint followed the muzzle/root. `bgunUpdateLasersight()` now transforms both near and far beam endpoints from the muzzle matrix/local forward vector before projection, while the steady-state gate keeps stale equip/busy beams hidden. Verification: focused mesh extractor test passed 80 assertions / 1 case, `.pdweapon` test passed 65 assertions / 1 case, `[bondgun][laser][static]` passed 25 assertions / 4 cases, queued `falconbeam5` all-target build passed, per-test `boot_smoke` passed 14/14 and wrote 303 `.pdmesh` plus 86 `.pdweapon` archives, Falcon OBJ reports 422 triangles / 4 display lists / 8 matrix commands / 8 model-matrix references with the previous severe-Y group absent, and the mixed severe-Y detached scan across generated weapon meshes found 0. Manual retest remains: inspect Falcon 2 in-game while equipping/moving/firing, and open the extracted Falcon 2 OBJ for corrected barrel-end placement.

**Asset Pipeline format priority update (2026-05-22; superseded by 2026-05-25 cleanup)**: Kanban `c3824` became the top Asset Pipeline migration plan, with `c3832` placed underneath to record the full target `.pdweapon` format before the extraction sweep. The target weapon archive uses `weapon.ini`, purpose folders (`models/`, `materials/`, `textures/`, `animations/`, `sounds/`, `behavior/`, `projectiles/`, `entities/`, `ui/`), and `_meta/` for machine-owned manifest/inventory/provenance/validation data. Authored mod weapons default to one model, hands are character-owned, material slots enable skin overrides, behavior has separate primary/secondary graph files plus shared settings/variables, `.pdmod` remains transport only, and import installs only missing or newer compatible typed dependency assets. This work is now closed through `c3824`, `c3838`, and `c3814`; `c3832` is Done.

**Asset Pipeline utility and node-editor priority update (2026-05-23)**: Kanban `c3834` now sits numerically behind the clean archive format decision/implementation cards at order `3000`. It owns the matching Modding Hub and command-line utility contracts for each typed archive family: create/import/clone/edit/validate/package flows, pickers/previews, template hydration, hot-enable/catalog rebuild behavior, self-contained payload preservation, `_meta/` handling, and catalog-name-only references. New Kanban card `c3835` sits immediately behind that Asset Pipeline block at order `4000` for the shared Blueprint-style node editor foundation: typed pin rendering, wires colored from the pins they connect, reusable graph model/render separation, shared context refs, inspector editing, validation UX, saved layouts, and future gameplay-script graph reuse. Process order is now `c3824` umbrella archive migration, `c3832` concrete `.pdweapon` format, `c3834` per-family mod utility designs, `c3835` reusable node editor foundation, then the broad extraction/examples/validators sweep after formats and utilities are frozen.

**Asset Pipeline format and two-zone validation update (2026-05-24)**: Kanban `c3824` is wrapped and moved to Done. All current typed or otherwise referenceable family layouts are frozen in [designs/modding/asset-archive-clean-formats.md](designs/modding/asset-archive-clean-formats.md). `asset_archive_policy` centralizes typed archive descriptors, migration readers prefer `_meta/manifest.json` but accept legacy root metadata, new emitters write manifests and hash sidecars under `_meta/`, `.pdmesh` release output uses `mesh.ini` with `model.ini` accepted only in migration mode, and `.pdmod` package validation rejects non-zip typed files, `.pdwpn`, authored `.bin`, missing root descriptors, root machine metadata, and unresolved recursive descriptor/GLTF/OBJ/MTL/JSON/nested-archive refs. `examples/modding/typed-pdxxx-basic` now covers every frozen family with `_meta/manifest.json`, and release packaging fails stale typed asset outputs before zipping if they are `.pdwpn`, non-zip, descriptor-less, legacy `.pdmesh` `model.ini`, root-metadata, or `.bin`-backed archives. No manual retest is required for this headless validator/examples/release-gate card.

**Asset Pipeline decision intake update (2026-05-24)**: Kanban now has an `Asset Decisions` tab for Mike to approve, change, request more info, defer, or reject each archive-format recommendation before implementation cards are updated. The phone/mobile portal exposes the tab through a compact top section selector while keeping the card-list-first workflow. Mike approved all 35 rows, and `c3824` records the final decisions. New first-class families are `.pdskin`, `.pdeffect`, `.pdprop`, `.pdvehicle`, `.pdmission`, `.pdgamemode`, `.pdbotprofile`, `.pdhud`, and `.pdtheme`; `.pdtool` is deferred from the current implementation sweep but high priority as a later secure tool/plugin package. Final clarifications: `.pdentity` is behavior/archetype-focused and can depend on `.pdprop`; `.pdmaterial` is classic-first but PBR-extensible while future PBR materials remain standalone assets; `.pdscenario` maps to new `ASSET_SCENARIO`; `.pdmesh` owns geometry/UVs while textures/materials stay typed dependencies; raw owned source files are allowed but cross-family resources embed as typed archives; validators fail unresolved references unless explicitly declared as base dependencies with on-disk/catalog provider source. `c3834` now includes utility-flow subtasks for the approved families, and the Asset Decisions tab is in needs-answer-only mode with no pending rows until new questions appear.

**Asset Pipeline extraction implementation handoff (2026-05-24)**: The post-decision self-containment sweep is recorded in [designs/modding/asset-archive-clean-formats.md](designs/modding/asset-archive-clean-formats.md), and the deeper modularization audit is recorded in [audits/asset-archive-modularization-verification-2026-05-24.md](audits/asset-archive-modularization-verification-2026-05-24.md). The planned authoring boundaries are conceptually sound, but end-to-end modularization is not verified until `c3838` closes catalog type/mapping mismatches, missing archive-policy rows, scanner/packer/distribution gaps, online manifest coverage, runtime utilization adapters, and the shared `_meta/manifest.json` dependency schema. The `.pdweapon` detail doc now treats `dependencies/assets/` typed archives as canonical for cross-family payloads; loose purpose folders are transition-reader input only. New Kanban card `c3838` is active at order `2500` for implementing the final extraction process.

**Asset Pipeline extraction writer progress (2026-05-24, c3838-s1)**: First verified implementation slice landed: `asset_archive_writer` centralizes root descriptor writes, `_meta/manifest.json`, inventory, hashes, provenance, validation, source handles, and public-file SHA sidecars, and `.pdlang` is the first base extractor wired through it. `c3838-s1` remains active until the remaining emitters use the shared writer contract.

**Asset Pipeline C-3838 closure (2026-05-25, c3838-s10)**: C-3838 is code/build/test verified for the asset-type migration, shared writer contract, descriptor/policy/scanner/distribution recognition, generic match-manifest coverage, dependency manifest/fallback validation, runtime binding surface, and external-file accessibility. Runtime/file-access coverage now includes typed public files through mounted `.pdmod` paths, `.pdskin` texture payload registration, `.pdprop` model payload binding with prop type/flags/health, metadata-family primary authored files for material/effect/HUD/vehicle/mission, scenario/theme/font/gamemode/bot-profile runtime use, and first-pass `.pdprojectile`/`.pdentity` graph runtime records from direct JSON, zip-openable archives, or catalog graph files loaded through the FileProvider/`fsFileLoad` path. Final verification passed `[modding][pdxxx][runtime][c3838][adapters]` 73 assertions / 3 cases, `[modding][pdxxx][runtime][c3838]` 269 assertions / 9 cases, `[modding][pdxxx]` 2784 assertions / 47 cases, and isolated `c3838done` all-target build. Weapon gameplay parity is now closed under `c3814` as the graph-runtime callsite consumption layer.

**Asset Pipeline native-source correction (done 2026-05-25, c3842)**: Mike clarified that the scenario `scene.glb`/runtime-source rule applies to every asset type and was the original Asset Pipeline intent. The global correction is now code/build/test verified for the current typed family set: every typed archive public payload is directly user-editable, and the game client utilizes those same source files through catalog/provider loading or deterministic source-derived cache. Final c3842 fixes closed the remaining descriptor-only runtime gaps: `.pdgamemode` now preserves and requires `rules_file`; `.pdbotprofile` now preserves and requires `profile_file` plus catalog-ID `target_body`; and base `.pdweapon` extraction no longer emits public `behavior/runtime.graph.json`, with runtime compilation composing `behavior/primary.graph.json` and `behavior/secondary.graph.json` instead. `c3840` remains the separate high-priority follow-up for one-by-one retirement/modularization of original weapon execution routines after parity proof.

**c3842 guard hooks (2026-05-25)**: The native editable-source contract now has Codex and repo guard rails. `AGENTS.md` contains the required Codex preflight for asset work; the PD2 large-change sweep skill has an Asset Pipeline c3842 gate; `tools/asset_native_source_guard.py` validates source-of-truth docs, Kanban state, hook wiring, and example typed archives; `.githooks/pre-commit` runs that guard with staged-file checks; and `[modding][pdxxx][c3842]` static tests pin the hook chain. The guard also rejects public `runtime.graph.json`, raw setup/mpsetup/visual dumps, authored `.bin` payloads, and numeric/legacy asset references in public archive text.

**Debug asset source gate (2026-05-27, c3842-s10)**: Settings > Debug now includes a persisted one-family-at-a-time asset source gate (`Debug.AssetSourceOnlyType`). When a family is selected, catalog typed lifecycle loading and the legacy file/texture/animation/sound gateways must use public FileProvider source for that family; ROM/static fallback is refused loudly so migration gaps are visible during playtest. Verification passed `asset_native_source_guard.py`, focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3842][debug][source_gate]` (31 assertions / 1 case), focused `[modding][pdxxx][c3842]` (133 assertions / 7 cases), and isolated `sourcegate` all-target build.

**HIGH PRIORITY ACTIVE 2026-05-27: Runtime ROM fallback is an asset-chain failure (`c3844`)**: Mike clarified that any need to load from ROM as a fallback after extraction means the asset ecosystem chain is broken. ROM is bootstrap/source input only; runtime loading must resolve to extracted typed archive source, FileProvider/catalog provider source, or deterministic source-derived cache. This card owns the global audit/removal of remaining fallback paths beyond the Debug source gate.

**FIXED 2026-05-28: Public table asset references now require actual catalog IDs (`c3844-s1`)**: Strict archive conformance now scans public `.tsv`/`.csv` asset-reference columns, including Scenario `objects.json` fields such as `model_catalog_id`, `weapon_catalog_id`, `body_catalog_id`, and `head_catalog_id`. The guard rejects raw numeric handles, legacy symbols, invalid catalog strings, and catalog-looking refs that do not resolve to declared root/nested catalog IDs. **PROGRESS 2026-05-28: Scenario setup fields now have a named public source table (`B-385`, `c3844-s1`)**: Generated `.pdscenario` archives now include required `setup.fields.json`, declared through `_meta/manifest.json` and `scenario.ini`, validated by strict conformance/native-source hooks, and bound by the scenario walker as `setup_fields_file`. It is a named per-command field table using catalog IDs, pad labels, and record IDs instead of raw setup words. This closes the summary-only `objects.json` authoring gap, but runtime setup still needs a compiler from this public source into `struct stagesetup`.

**PROGRESS 2026-05-28: Scenario and Mission graph source activation is runtime-backed (`B-385`, `c3844-s1`)**: Stage load now activates and validates `.pdscenario::level.graph.json` before setup fallback, and solo stage load activates the matching `.pdmission::mission.graph.json` by schema plus scenario reference. Chicago smoke proved both public archive-member graph files are accessible through `fsFileLoad()` at runtime. This is not full behavior parity yet: mission/level graph execution still needs parity cutover beyond graph-source activation, and source-only mode must not be made to pass by exposing raw setup words, `.bin` cache, or fake FileProvider handles.

**PROGRESS 2026-05-28: Scenario level graph table refs now select runtime source files (`B-385`, `c3844-s1`)**: Runtime source loaders now bind the active `level.graph.json` table refs for pads, setup fields, objects, objectives, waypoints, waygroups, and covers, then prefer those graph-selected public archive-member paths during stage load. Chicago smoke proved the table-ref path before compiling pads/navigation. This keeps the graph from being documentation-only while behavior execution parity remains open.

**FIXED 2026-05-28: Mission objective graph nodes now feed runtime objective insertion/evaluation/checks (`B-385`, `c3844-s1`)**: Generated `.pdmission` archives now mirror decoded scenario objective rows as per-objective and per-criteria graph nodes (`mission.objective.source` / `mission.objective.criteria.source`) plus an explicit parity-backend node. `objectives.tsv` points each row at its mission graph node and scenario source row, stale mission archives are regenerated by the `pdmeta_table_backed_v4` cache/content checks, runtime validation rejects empty/generic objective graphs, `objectiveInsert()` validates the inserted objective against the public graph row/difficulty/criteria sequence, active mission graphs drive objective criteria evaluation order, and `objectiveCheck()` records against the active graph. Follow-ups moved mutable Enter Room, Throw In Room, Holograph, COMPFLAGS/FAILFLAGS mission-stage flags, and tagged objective object state (present/healthy/held by mission player) into graph-owned runtime state, so objective evaluation now logs `backend=graph.objective.operands+graph.objective.state+graph.objective.object_state+graph.mission.flags` instead of reading OG criteria/object/inventory/flag state directly. **FIXED 2026-05-31: CI texture tuple crash and menu HUD-piece source chain (`B-412`/`B-413`, `c3844`)**: The latest local client exception path was the fast3d `Bad size for RGBA texture in tile 0: 00` fatal during the first CI/font render. **FIXED 2026-05-28: MP setup AI-list sort no longer corrupts source-compiled pads (`B-386`, `c3844-s1`)**: The strict DY-357 match smoke exposed that the source pad buffer could be overwritten after `setupLoadFiles()`. Root cause was the stage AI-list sort scanning past the converted MP setup AI-list table and swapping entries until a stray NULL appeared, which could write into the next stage-pool allocation. `setupLoadFiles()` now bounds conversion/sorting/counting by the loaded setup blob size and `setupPreparePads()` keeps padfile-size validation for waypoint, waygroup, and cover pointer promotion. Base `.pdhead`/`.pdbody` public descriptors use catalog IDs and typed mesh archives instead of `headnum`/`bodynum`/`FILE_*`, and base `.pdweapon` public graphs/descriptors now use catalog IDs for model/audio/animation bindings instead of numeric or legacy symbols. Verification passed the strict checker on checked-in examples, the native-source guard, focused archive/runtime tests, isolated all-target build, boot smoke with the verified binary, and strict conformance on the fresh smoke-extracted archive set: 3,503 root archives / 4,434 total archives checked.

**Gameplay polish note (2026-05-23)**: Tiny Mode generic-enemy tripling is code/build/test verified. Stage setup now counts two extra chr slots for each qualifying non-unique enemy when `CHEAT_SMALLJO` is active, creates the original first, then creates two fresh-id tiny copies offset around the original spawn using pad-facing left/right candidates and collision-checked fallback placement so the three enemies do not start inside each other. The predicate is intentionally narrow: solo setup chrs only, `TEAM_ENEMY`, `SPAWNFLAG_BASICGUARD`, no invincible/conversation/chair/clone-hearspawn/Chuck Norris/story-body cases. Follow-up cleanup makes `CHEAT_SMALLJO` the only visible/runtime small-character cheat, hides the old `CHEAT_SMALLCHARACTERS` menu row as a legacy id, labels the row `Tiny Mode`, and fixes controller activation for focused cheat rows plus `Unlock All...`. Additional speed pass marks those tiny generic enemies with `CHRCFLAG_TINYMODE_MOVESPEED` and applies a `1.3x` movement animation-speed multiplier across core locomotion and dodge paths. Contract correction keeps the player normal-sized by removing Tiny Mode from player body scale, first-person camera/movement scaling, player shadow scaling, and shelf-pickup range. Verification: scoped diff check passed, focused `[audio][voice][tiny][static],[cheats][tiny][spawn][static]` passed 55 assertions / 5 cases, focused `[cheats][tiny][spawn][static]` speed coverage passed 54 assertions / 4 cases, focused player-normal coverage passed 67 assertions / 5 cases, queued `tinyspread`, `tinyfast`, and `tinynoplayer` all-target builds passed, and session builds were removed. Manual retest: enable Tiny Mode, confirm the player stays normal size/camera/movement, load a guard-heavy mission, confirm ordinary enemies appear as three spaced tiny enemies, move about 30% faster than before, and named/story characters stay single; also confirm controller A can toggle rows and open/confirm Unlock All.

**Modding note (2026-05-20)**: External-format content plus `.pdmod` transport card `c3809` is done. Corrected packaging split is implemented: typed `*.pdxxx` files are the preferred content units for organization; `.pdmod` is the bundle/transport envelope for sharing, Public Mods, and online-required content delivery; authored `.bin` files are invalid in both content units and transport archives; generated engine-native cache is private, readable, rebuildable, and unshipped. Runtime validation is complete for legacy `.pd*` registration, loose typed `*.pdxxx` folder content, typed content wrapped in real `.pdmod` transport, and Public Mods installed `.pdmod` discovery/install. Final closure also fixed archive/folder digest comparison to root `mod.json` bytes and refreshes `g_ModRegistry` after received/distributed mod installs. Follow-up card `c3810` is done: request-download installs from friends hot-enable after validated install; non-friend downloads install disabled and queue a Social-shell modal to enable now or keep disabled. Focused Public Mods static tests and queued `modreq` all-target build passed.

**Modding sample/export gap (2026-05-20; closed; no-public-TSV rule updated 2026-06-07)**: Kanban `c3812` repaired c3811's wrong archive shape. Mike clarified the actual contract: each typed `*.pdxxx` file is itself the modder-facing asset archive. Changing `.pdhead`, `.pdarena`, `.pdanim`, etc. to `.zip` should reveal the descriptor, meshes, textures, INI/JSON/graph metadata, audio, and other authored source files needed to edit or clone that asset. `.pdmod` remains only the networking/Public Mods/online transport wrapper. Authored `.bin` payloads and public TSV final source are invalid; runtime cache may be generated but must be readable, private, rebuildable, and unshipped. c3811's loose descriptor plus same-name folder wording is superseded by c3812.

**c3812 progress (2026-05-20)**: Scanner/runtime nested archive support is done. Folder mods and `.pdmod` transport archives scan typed `*.pdxxx` archive entries directly, and runtime paths such as `heads/tri_head.pdhead::model.gltf` resolve through VFS without extraction. `modVfsCanResolve`, `modVfsGetSize`, and `modVfsResolveAnyAlloc` now understand nested typed archive paths so preflight checks do not rewrite them as loose filesystem paths. Permanent examples are real zip-openable typed archives, and the docs/UI/context wording pass now presents `.pdxxx` as the editable asset archive and `.pdmod` as transport only. Mike clarified the strict bar: every asset archive must be fully self-contained, including model textures/UV material references, rig/mesh linkage, animation targets, weapon model/animation/audio relationships, and any other authored dependencies. Follow-up correction 2026-05-21: weapon behavior uses `.pdweapon` only; `.pdwpn` is fully deprecated, was never released, and must be removed rather than accepted, aliased, migrated, or supported for compatibility. The format must cover trigger-pulled fire cadence in centiseconds, custom projectiles, rapid/looping fire, hold beams, charge/release, secondary modes, melee, zoom, reticle/overlay/camera effects, and ammo-driven model or screen state. Automated archive coverage opens current example families, verifies required inner authored files, and checks descriptor/GLTF/OBJ references resolve inside the same archive; weapon coverage must move to `.pdweapon`. Focused c3812 all-asset archive tests and adjacent c3809 modding tests pass.

**Asset archive self-containment clarification (2026-05-21)**: Mike reaffirmed that the strict bar applies to every typed asset, including weapons: opening one asset archive must expose every authored dependency required to edit, clone, share, and load that asset. Extra disk duplication is acceptable. Catalog building/runtime loading may dedupe duplicate inner assets by SHA-256 and catalog identity to limit RAM waste after ingestion, but on-disk archives must not rely on external reference-only dependencies. Archive dependency closure is now complete for the non-weapon `c3812` target set and the `.pdweapon` packaging/export path under `c3814`; `c3814` remains open for runtime behavior parity/adapters, not asset self-containment.

Combat Simulator Custom weapon slots now display a sorted catalog-native ASSET_WEAPON list, so saved/enabled mod weapons without authored numeric `weapon_id` can appear by catalog name. Match setup/network read paths preserve `weapon_ids[]` and avoid casting catalog-only weapons to legacy `255`; manifests include catalog-native custom slots for distribution; Modding Hub weapon and debug arena lists were sorted; and No Score Limit now stores the runtime unlimited sentinel (`scorelimit=100`). **Mod weapon match-start crash note (2026-05-23, B-371)**: Latest build client log showed a crash starting a match with `mod:weapon_dxw_shotgun` selected in custom weapon slots. The PC symbolized to `modelPromoteNodeOffsetsToPointers()` because `.pdweapon` was being loaded through the raw modeldef lifecycle path. Fixed now: `ASSET_WEAPON` manifest loads use metadata/runtime activation, COMPONENT entries validate through the Mod Manager instead of the asset catalog, disabled/none/shield MP weapon slots are skipped from active-set manifests, and catalog-only custom weapon slots disable stale legacy MP slots until full saved-custom runtime binding lands. **c3812 non-weapon base archive slice (done 2026-05-21)**: Base `.pdarena`, `.pdhead`, and `.pdbody` extraction no longer emits plain JSON files with typed extensions. Those emitters now write zip-openable typed archives containing their root descriptor (`arena.ini`, `head.ini`, `body.ini`) plus compatibility `manifest.json` for the current universal walker. Existing legacy JSON outputs are treated as stale and rewritten even without a force flag. The already-zip non-weapon base emitters now include root descriptors too: `.pdmesh` -> `model.ini`, character `.pdanim` -> `animation.ini`, `.pdsfx` -> `sound.ini`, `.pdvoice` -> `voice.ini`, `.pdsong` -> `music.ini`, `.pdui` -> `ui.ini`, `.pdfont` -> `font.ini`, `.pdlang` -> `lang.ini`, and `.pdscenario` -> `scenario.ini`; descriptor-less existing zips are treated as stale. Follow-up extractor work added canonical `.pdcharacter` archives with `character.ini`, `manifest.json`, and nested `.pdbody`/`.pdhead` dependency archives; scanner, packer, and allowed typed-archive suffixes now recognize `.pdcharacter`. `.pdhead` and `.pdbody` remain lower-level dependency/runtime compatibility archives, not the top-level character asset name. The base `.pdsfx`/`.pdvoice` walker now decodes ALADPCM/RAW16 ROM sample data into accessible mono PCM16 `sample.wav` files with `sample.wav.sha256`; `sound.ini`/`voice.ini` and `manifest.json` point at `sample.wav` and retain `source_format` provenance. The `.pdlang` extractor now decodes raw ROM language offset tables into editable `strings.tsv` with `strings.tsv.sha256`; `lang.ini` and `manifest.json` point at `strings.tsv`, and old `data.bin`-only language archives are stale. The `.pdfont` extractor now decodes ROM font segments into `glyphs.pgm`, `metrics.tsv`, and `kerning.tsv` with SHA-256 sidecars; `font.ini` and `manifest.json` point at those files, and old `data.bin`-only font archives are stale. The `.pdsong` extractor now inflates RareZip N64 compressed-MIDI sequences, header-swaps them, and emits standard `sequence.mid` plus editable `sequence.tsv` event listings with SHA-256 sidecars; `music.ini` and `manifest.json` point at those files, and old `data.bin`-only music archives are stale. Character `.pdanim` archives now emit editable `header.tsv` and `frames.tsv` instead of `frames.bin`, with SHA-256 sidecars. `.pdmesh` archives now promote the source `PD_MODELDEF`, walk model display lists, and emit standard Wavefront `model.obj` plus `model.mtl` and SHA-256 sidecars instead of `geometry.bin` or a TSV byte table. `.pdscenario` now emits standard `rooms.obj` plus `scenario.mtl`, decoded `tiles.tsv`, decoded `pads.tsv`, and visual OBJ/MTL/TGA payloads instead of raw `geometry.bin`/`tiles.bin`/`pads.bin`/`setup.bin`/`mpsetup.bin`; the later c3842 correction also removed raw public setup/mpsetup/visual word dumps until c3841 replaces them with decoded catalog-ID tables and graphs. Focused static coverage pins the non-weapon descriptor set, `.pdcharacter` wiring, WAV payload guard, TSV language guard, bitmap-font guard, MIDI/event song guard, character animation TSV guard, `.pdmesh` OBJ guard, and `.pdscenario` standard map/text payload guard. Verification: `pdxasset` client build passed; focused `[modding][pdxxx][base][static][c3812]` passed 212 assertions / 11 cases. Weapon graph archive work is handled by c3814.

**c3812 extractor crash hardening (done 2026-05-21)**: B-357 fixed the first-launch asset extractor crash class surfaced by `modelPromoteNodeOffsetsToPointers` / `modelPromoteOffsetsToPointers`. `.pdmesh` now RareZip-inflates ROM payloads before model/gun preprocessing, promotes skeleton type before offsets, validates promotable model offsets before the node walk, and handles low-bit display-list flags before OBJ traversal. `.pdarena` / `.pdscenario` stage payload extraction now also inflates before tiles/pads/setup preprocessing. Both extractors run their preprocessor-dependent work serially because those preprocessors use process-global marker/GBI scratch state. Verification: focused `[modding][pdxxx][base][static][c3812]` passed 229 assertions / 11 cases, queued `meshprom` all-target build passed, and `boot_smoke` passed from a clean smoke install.

**c3812 dependency-closure pass (done for non-weapons 2026-05-21; inspection-output cleanup 2026-05-26)**: The non-weapon archive closure audit is complete. `.pdarena` now embeds the corresponding `.pdscenario` public source files under `scenario/`; `.pdhead` embeds `mesh.pdmesh`; `.pdbody` embeds `mesh.pdmesh` and optional `hand.pdmesh`; `.pdcharacter` refreshes nested body/head archives and marks `dependency_closure = embedded.v2`. `.pdmesh` work dedupe is now keyed by `(filenum,hint)`, so body/hand mesh archive identities are not suppressed by weapon hi/lo jobs. Follow-up B-373 removes stale `.zip` inspection siblings before arena/scenario/mesh fast-cache skips, keeps embedded arena scenario folders from growing duplicate nested `_meta` sidecar trees, versions the clean arena/scenario fast-cache stamps, and rejects old `scenario/_meta/`, `_meta/scenario/`, and `_meta/_meta/` entries so long-lived Build outputs self-regenerate. Release validation now rejects typed-family `.zip` inspection copies and loose extracted archive folders. The canonical generated output is the `.pdarena` / `.pdscenario` / `.pdmesh` file, not a same-name `.zip` or extracted folder left beside it. Verification: `assetcl` all-target build passed and `assetc3` focused `[modding][pdxxx][base][static][c3812]` passed 283 assertions / 12 cases for the original closure; B-373 passed focused guard/release tests, isolated all-target builds, patched-client `boot_smoke`, and a fresh smoke install audit with zero bad scenarios, zero stale zip inspection copies, and zero loose typed archive folders. Weapon archive closure is recorded under `c3814`.

**c3812 BG visual map authoring payload (done for non-weapons 2026-05-21)**: The previous neutral glTF/map_material placeholder is replaced by a proper BG visual display-list exporter. `.pdscenario` now emits `visual/scene.obj`, `visual/scene.mtl`, `visual/materials.tsv`, `visual/export_version.txt`, and decoded `visual/textures/*.tga` from BG C0/G_NOOP material commands, `G_VTX`, `G_TRI1`, `G_TRI4`, and `textureslist`/`texturesdata`; `.pdarena` embeds the same payload under `scenario/`. `rooms.obj` remains the runtime/collision OBJ path, while `visual/scene.obj` is the plugin-free Blender import path with `scene.mtl` linking textures via Blender-relative `map_Kd textures/...` paths. `materials.tsv` records material state, triangle counts, decoded dimensions, and unused recovered texture inventory rows. Old `.pdscenario` and embedded `.pdarena` scenario archives without `visual/export_version.txt` are stale and regenerate. Verification: focused `[modding][pdxxx][base][static][c3812]` passed 311 assertions / 12 cases, focused `[modding][pdmod][static][c3809]` passed 520 assertions / 13 cases, queued `bgvis` all-target build passed, `boot_smoke` passed, and clean extracted Chicago archives contain `scene.obj`, `scene.mtl`, `materials.tsv`, `export_version.txt`, and 98 TGA textures in both embedded `.pdarena` and standalone `.pdscenario` forms. c3812 is done for the non-weapon target set; `.pdweapon` closure is handled separately under c3814.

**Weapon graph asset design/audit (2026-05-21; closed 2026-05-25)**: Kanban `c3814` is closed for weapon behavior graph runtime parity. The implemented surface now covers `.pdweapon`/`.pdprojectile`/`.pdentity` schema and archive layouts, graph node categories and IR boundary, validation gates, current weapon behavior-family mapping, named weapon/projectile/entity behavior modules, `.pdweapon` code cutover, first-class `.pdprojectile`/`.pdentity` catalog and manifest metadata, graph archive helpers, base graph emission, graph validation, deterministic IR compilation, Debug Settings graph-runtime toggle storage, Mods > Weapons browser/template/save flows, the held/player/AI runtime adapter, modular shared-context authoring, in-game node editor, projectile/entity runtime records, gameplay callsite consumption, and sight/zoom presentation values. **C-3814 weapon graph runtime parity closure (2026-05-25)**: The earlier held/runtime adapter work is now complete through projectile, deployed-entity, and presentation callsites. **c3814-s20 Mods Weapons browser done (2026-05-21)**: The Modding Hub now has a Weapons tab that lists catalog weapon assets, marks Base vs Mod entries, shows catalog/model/runtime/archive details, and previews `weapon.ini`, `behavior.graph.json`, and `nested_payloads.json` when the `.pdweapon` archive is resolvable. The compiler preserves and hashes shared contexts, subgraphs, and per-node `subgraph` ownership; the base emitter writes those fields; and the Modding Hub graph builder now has a Shared Context panel, mode-scoped module insertion, per-node mode assignment, and seeds for dual fire modes, remote mine/detonator linkage, and Laptop Gun targeting control. **c3814-s25 node editor interaction fix (2026-05-23, B-370)**: Mike reported that weapon behavior graph nodes did not show usable connection wires, links could not be created, and specific node options did not seem editable. First pass enlarged exec socket hit targets, kept drag-to-connect, added an inspector `Connect To` / `Connect Selected` fallback, and rendered simple JSON params as typed controls before Advanced JSON. Second pass fixed the remaining "not connected or no wires" path: base weapon templates now regenerate as `base_weapon_graph_v2` with explicit trigger-entry nodes connected to primary/secondary action nodes, graph presets create trigger-to-action edges, imported graphs without saved `editor.layout` get deterministic fallback positions, links render as thicker wires, and the inspector lists current incoming/outgoing links. Third/fourth pass corrected pin placement and color: a fixed-width four-column ImGui table now docks input socket/label on the left and output label/socket on the right, node-editor pivots attach wires to the outside socket edges, and exec pins/wires are white while still deriving wire color from the pin type. The creator has `Details`, `Assets`, `Primary Graph`, `Secondary Graph`, and `Payloads` tabs; template startup auto-populates available model, animation, audio, projectile, and entity refs from `weapon.ini`, nested typed-archive descriptors, manifests, and graph params. **Extraction audit follow-up (resolved 2026-05-21)**: B-358 is fixed. The Combat Sim crash run did not show core ROM/segment extraction failures (`ROMEXTRACT failed=0`, segment verify `failed=0`), but it did expose archive/fallback misses above the summary counters. Weapon/inventory `.pdanim` extraction now emits zip-openable archives with `animation.ini`, compatibility `manifest.json`, and editable `opcodes.json` instead of loose JSON. `.pdmesh` extraction now masks low-bit GDL/vertex markers, decodes `G_VTX` with the runtime microcode layout, resolves segment-4 vertex addresses from the node vertex buffer, and no longer silently skips no-triangle OBJ failures. The raw menu HUD piece filenum `0x0259` now has a catalog provider, local/offline Combat Simulator builds an MP manifest before stage change instead of falling into `MANIFEST-SP: late-add`, and random bot bodies exclude SP-only rows like `base:sp_body_108`. Latest `combat_sim_entry` smoke from isolated `b316arch` reported ROM/segment failures at zero, `.pdmesh written=64 skipped=192 failed=0`, `.pdanim skipped=110 failed=0`, no `CATALOG.MISS`, no `MANIFEST-SP: late-add`, and no exception/fatal. The remaining `pd-modern-ui` / `mod:base-ui` theme fallback in isolated smoke is a user-pref/mod install fallback, not a base extractor miss.

Benchmark/GPU swarm work and vague failed-test cleanup are demoted behind the ship-critical chain. `c083` / `B-316` is code/build/smoke verified and pending Mike's exact Grid 1-player / 8-bot / Bot Jumping playtest: bot target prop derefs now prove live prop-pool range and `prop->chr` / `chr->prop` backlinks before attack/follow/protect/target/jump paths use them.
